# Actual test execution ledger

## OpenAPI staff accounts and recovery · 22 November 2026

The local PostgreSQL suite passed nine tests in 23.747 seconds and observed 27 successful schema-checked operations, including the 26 staff additions. It verified same-origin CSRF, an original synthetic recovery email and expired replay, password change, cross-team refusal, scoped invitation renewal, exact role admission, unchanged action deadline, and restored sign-in while prior roles and grants remain revoked. OpenAPI 3.1 validation, TypeScript generation and migration drift passed.

Railway checks `8094ceaa-a2a4-48e6-bdd3-c318ff380086` on `af299509` passed all 17 PostgreSQL tests in 16.613 seconds. The schema audit recorded 28 successful operations, including all 26 additions, plus the expected rejected anonymous session read. Railway system checks and migration drift passed. [Staff API checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=8094ceaa-a2a4-48e6-bdd3-c318ff380086)

Design acceptance web deployment `27a69446-038a-48b0-8e4e-70676c3edf54` served the exact 1.7.0 snapshot. Health and Django sign-in returned 200. The session bootstrap returned signed-out state, and `/api/v1/me` returned 401. The worker upload response timed out after submission. Railway had accepted deployment `c119c359-7421-4d50-993a-dfc2c923e223` from the same frozen revision and logs confirmed queue polling. [Hosted contract](https://psl-design-web-design-acceptance.up.railway.app/api/v1/openapi.json)

## OpenAPI recovery and release closeout · 22 November 2026

The first four PostgreSQL checks passed locally in 94.300 seconds, including original payroll replacement, separately attested names, an idempotent stopped-work restart, and reminder policy through mailbox receipt. The first Go-live and contract batch passed six of seven cases in 165.418 seconds. The stability case reached verified work, then an assertion accidentally carried over from the preceding Django missing-configuration test expected no assessment. That assertion was restored to its original test and the stability test now checks the qualified result it produced.

Railway's first upload for revision `4aab08fc` timed out and created failed deployment `d5b27b43-2ba2-4a55-b5d6-c21eea563374`. Retrying the same frozen bundle as `fa465552-168a-43d4-a648-e83e51fa1395` passed all nine recovery checks in 186.236 seconds and all four Go-live checks in 118.717 seconds. The response audit recorded 18 and five successful operations respectively. Django system checks and migration drift passed. [Railway checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=fa465552-168a-43d4-a648-e83e51fa1395)

## OpenAPI HR handoffs and payroll questions · 22 November 2026

The first combined local run passed the two HR disclosure tests and four contract checks. One payroll question test returned 503 on the assigned HR reader's exact view. The report read was validating its source lineage twice. The narrower path uses the exact review for lineage and authority checks and omits the aggregate amount payload for question metadata. The revised local suite passed all 11 tests in 80.797 seconds, with successful responses for all twelve new operations. It checked rejected and corrected HR exports, the distinct Finance recipient, exact aggregate receipt, scoped reply and original source-owner revocation. The existing Django two-report test deferred at its configured read limit after 1,116 queries, then opened the exact question separately.

The final seven-test local rerun passed in 45.623 seconds after adding explicit API queue deferral. Railway checks deployment `b97fdb58-c290-42eb-98d4-9b67afecb0f3` on `6b9291ee` passed all 11 tests in 130.330 seconds. The schema audit recorded 12 successful operations. System checks, migration drift, official OpenAPI validation and TypeScript generation passed. Data, HR authority and destinations were synthetic. [Railway HR checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=b97fdb58-c290-42eb-98d4-9b67afecb0f3)

Railway web deployment `eaeb5042-a3bb-4cec-b4a6-5e0d7541977e` on `6b9291ee` served the exact OpenAPI 1.5.0 snapshot. Django sign-in and health returned 200. The signed-out session endpoint returned 401. The same revision booted on worker deployment `63995c22-c580-4657-bc30-2004cf0017f4` and polled the handoff and publication queues. [Hosted schema](https://psl-design-web-design-acceptance.up.railway.app/api/v1/openapi.json)

## OpenAPI Delivery and restricted payroll · 22 November 2026

The first combined local run revealed over-budget reads in Planning, stability and the archive, an oversized Go-live read in a separate run, and an enum serialisation mismatch. The initial snapshot comparison also ran while new routes were being generated. The revised reads use the narrow native dossier or packet context. Six local tests passed in 237.356 seconds, including the previously held stability and archive reads and the contract checks. Go-live reply and successor preparation passed on the next local run. The two final Planning tests passed in 188.344 seconds after the projection was narrowed.

The first Railway Delivery API deployment `8f33d9c0-e2f4-43bb-a48d-2ddeec4b08a2` passed 18 of 19 tests in 609.286 seconds. The remaining test hit the Planning read limit. The audit validated 28 successful API operations in that batch. The final Planning projection cut redundant packet and UI reads and was then tested in the local run above. [Railway checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=8f33d9c0-e2f4-43bb-a48d-2ddeec4b08a2)

The initial payroll and contract batch passed six local PostgreSQL tests in 20.721 seconds with six schema-valid successful operations. It verified original CSV and Excel import, a held unknown amount, nominee-scoped authoring, page-sized private rows and revoked access. Held draft calculation remained separate from publication. Type generation and OpenAPI 3.1 schema validation passed.

The two local payroll follow-up tests passed in 274.862 seconds. The assigned matcher acknowledged the exact authorised HR snapshot. A separately reviewed withdrawal retained the original recipient receipt, and the corrected successor required fresh reviews before publication. The local appointment and contract suite passed five tests in 4.218 seconds after correcting a test assertion to use the policy pin returned by native decision authority.

The isolated Railway rerun `ae56e7b8-c0d8-4c70-9175-1479d19f1c40` passed both Planning tests in 285.150 seconds and all eight restricted payroll tests in 522.765 seconds. The schema audit checked six successful Planning operations and seven successful payroll operations. Django system checks and migration drift passed. Setup deployment `d5301b6a-8830-4612-8e7c-2ed3c3ad5836` passed all five tests in 3.909 seconds, with four successful operations. [Planning and payroll checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=ae56e7b8-c0d8-4c70-9175-1479d19f1c40) · [Setup checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=d5301b6a-8830-4612-8e7c-2ed3c3ad5836)

The original bid receipt and generator checks passed five local tests in 3.627 seconds. The bid test compared the original bytes through the existing Django download, preserved its receipt after a successor, and checked reader permissions and revoked uploader authority. The first run reached the download and then failed because the test client closed the PostgreSQL connection while consuming the streamed response. The test performs the permission checks before the final download.

Final Railway deployment `aa1be800-8182-4072-915b-fb2ae119d9c8` on `522415df` passed all six Setup, original bid and contract tests in 2.591 seconds. The schema audit checked six successful operations. The three isolated Railway deployments together have successful response evidence for all 37 operations added in OpenAPI 1.4.0. Cases and responses span several revisions, with the Planning read-limit failure superseded by the passing rerun. OpenAPI 3.1 validation, TypeScript generation, Django checks and migration drift passed on the final revision. The browser ledger audit returned 262 completed scoped Django journeys. Full end-to-end acceptance of all 262 states using the new APIs remains open. [Final checks deployment](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=aa1be800-8182-4072-915b-fb2ae119d9c8)

Web deployment `2caac9eb-e808-42ff-ba17-d1c09c85cdff` on `522415df` served the exact OpenAPI 1.4.0 contract. Web health and Django sign-in returned 200. The signed-out session API returned 401. [Hosted schema](https://psl-design-web-design-acceptance.up.railway.app/api/v1/openapi.json)

## OpenAPI project records and recovery · 22 November 2026

The isolated local PostgreSQL batch passed all 11 tests in 84.374 seconds. The response audit covered 33 of 141 operations, with 31 successful responses. It exercised original source selection and revocation, retrospective and action authoring, independent client link review, private minutes upload and original receipt readback, controlled report rework and publication, opaque access requests, invoice proof and the exact pre-issue stop. An earlier run exposed a test assertion using `id` where the contract returns `work_id`. The assertion was corrected and the flow passed. External effects used synthetic adapters. The OpenAPI 3.1 schema and TypeScript generation passed.

The final minutes recovery run and four contract tests passed in 34.058 seconds. The minutes test verified a held delivery retried using the original job and checked both receipts through the API. The response audit covered five successful operations in this run, including the newly exercised retry command. Together these runs checked successful responses for every operation added in 1.3.0.

Railway deployment `5bb381fb-ed68-450f-bafc-ffe41576d6a9` passed system checks and migration drift detection. Django rejected the 66-character isolated database name before any tests. The replacement suite uses `project-api`, which keeps the complete database name within PostgreSQL’s 63-character limit. [First checks deployment](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=5bb381fb-ed68-450f-bafc-ffe41576d6a9)

Railway deployment `f736209f-2bc4-4f92-ae3f-d3789dc0e328` on `a839c5ac` passed system checks, migration drift and all 15 tests in 91.367 seconds. The schema audit recorded 32 successful operations, including every new command. A final local PostgreSQL run added the exact superseded client coverage check and matched the Django fallback for inaccessible alternate invoice evidence. All eight tests passed in 59.707 seconds, with 17 schema-checked successful operations. OpenAPI validation and TypeScript generation passed against the final snapshot. [Verified checks deployment](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=f736209f-2bc4-4f92-ae3f-d3789dc0e328)

Web deployment `fa07fea0-9ba0-4803-a85c-1a6cfcd9a235` on `1d180859` served the exact OpenAPI 1.3.0 snapshot. Health and Django sign-in returned 200. The signed-out session endpoint returned 401. Local migration drift passed against the isolated PostgreSQL test database. [Hosted schema](https://psl-design-web-design-acceptance.up.railway.app/api/v1/openapi.json)

## Workflow API cutover · 22 November 2026

The Windows frontend verification passed 441 focused tests, then all 3,629 tests in 151 files. Both previously timed-out test files ran in the focused and full passes. TypeScript passed. The fixes cover shell-specific source checks, path separators, paging retries and test worker startup.

The first PostgreSQL batch exposed a route shadowing the existing Milestones tab and a stale test expectation for revoked template access. The route moved to the exact completion subresource. The template test now checks the existing hidden-tab response. A later bank test caught exponential notation for zero remaining cash. The API now returns fixed-point decimal strings. The first template download test used the wrong storage mock target, and the milestone fixture carried an unrelated superseding role policy.

The four-test PostgreSQL rerun passed in 46.260 seconds and checked ten successful HTTP responses against the contract. It verified report-to-verified-lot-to-allocation, private template upload and download, new milestone to a Finance handoff, and independent invoice review comments. A separate three-test PostgreSQL run passed in 18.046 seconds for an unknown bank amount successor, payment search and an exact scoped client group. Both used synthetic data and isolated PostgreSQL databases.

The first Railway cutover check reached Django system checks and migration drift detection. The isolated test settings rejected the new suite name. The suite is now explicitly admitted on the checks service.

The first Delivery run passed Go-live through two independently declared signatures and a verified outcome. Planning exit and Support receipt initially exhausted the read budget while building the broader dashboard projections. The narrowed endpoints rechecked their original evidence and authority. Both PostgreSQL reruns passed in 169.275 seconds with four successful request and response validations, including stale write protection.

Across the local runs, the response validator exercised successful responses for all 42 added operations and 72 of the 115 total operations. The four contract tests passed, including the binding check for all 17 correction request variants. The closure correction ran end to end through a new review. The other correction variants were checked against their native service signatures. Django system checks, migration drift, OpenAPI 3.1 validation and TypeScript client generation passed. Storage and mail effects used synthetic fixtures and test adapters.

The worker supervisor test passed on Ubuntu in 3.506 seconds. It deliberately blocked both model queues and observed the effects and native progression commands start within the same probe. Railway web `fc17c996-4477-44c6-85c8-82fd3f0f29a3` returned the exact checked-in OpenAPI 1.2.0 document, health 200, the Django sign-in page 200 and the signed-out session API 401. Worker `e6cd2c07-be4f-4969-924c-10f4e058069d` started source readers and polled the application queues on the matching application revision `649ebec3`.

Railway cutover checks deployment `3223343f-ba9a-4664-935e-6ba219844a73` passed Django system checks, migration drift and all 34 isolated PostgreSQL tests in 1,097.695 seconds. The API audit observed schema-valid successful responses for 71 of the 115 operations. [Railway checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=3223343f-ba9a-4664-935e-6ba219844a73)

## OpenRouter coordinator and department assistants · 22 November 2026

Railway `e32a1640-5a4b-4884-9de7-36628d7b7c02` passed the five PostgreSQL checks with simulated model responses in 82.673 seconds, then passed all three live workflow tests in 111.000 seconds. The coordinator used `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017` for routing and report review. The runs checked all eight specialist roles across nine private report files and three business receipts. Tola cited the controlled publication, Chidi cited the approved trial-balance mapping, and Zainab cited Finance’s acknowledgement of the HR-approved handoff. For the HR handoff, the live coordinator raised four findings for the information reviewer before the independent approval. The UI and API checks used authenticated requests to Django and test data in an isolated Railway PostgreSQL database. [Initial hosted checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=e32a1640-5a4b-4884-9de7-36628d7b7c02)

Final Railway run `f1bd8eac-2dc1-4ac0-ac31-a4163fc3ed49` passed five PostgreSQL checks with simulated model responses in 64.503 seconds and all three live workflow tests in 95.933 seconds. The live coordinator selected approved source lines for every role and checked the nine reports read from private storage. Four reports reached supported findings and five identified insufficient evidence. The information reviewer handled those gaps and the coordinator’s additional findings in the Finance and publication cases. A separate authorised test principal recorded each business approval. The three assistants cited their exact downstream receipt. The isolated database and its private test-file versions were removed, and both temporary model references were verified absent. [Final hosted checks](https://railway.com/project/f957481b-c6f7-43af-bc66-449a330a9d53/service/00c14ca1-5361-4bee-97a5-2ec7aba6ef18?id=f1bd8eac-2dc1-4ac0-ac31-a4163fc3ed49)

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Chidi | F02 trial-balance mapping, station receipt 6 | Intake, investigation, Finance analysis | 1 |
| Zainab | F35 acknowledgement of HR-authorised Finance handoff, station receipt 7 | Document analysis, release preflight, workflow coordination | 0 |
| Tola | P52 controlled publication, station receipt 8 | Quality review, document interpretation, management drafting | 1 |

Application revision `8346a89c` is deployed to Railway design acceptance. Web `41e18fbf-1b00-482f-b11b-062bcac0c2dc` applied both agents migrations and returned 200 for health, sign-in and the API schema. Worker `f3c5049e-3b89-4aa9-aed9-75ec72fb0a4a` reported success and started two source readers. Six focused local PostgreSQL tests passed in 25.585 seconds for the OpenRouter configuration form, explicit source opt-in and revocation, model response validation and protected retry. The saved routing book still matches all 122 source processes.

The historical-planner and coordinator recovery suite passed 24/24 on local PostgreSQL in 860.254 seconds. The first archived-request fixture omitted the original work allowance and PostgreSQL rejected it. The corrected fixture reserves the native allowance before writing the historical request. The passing suite verifies that new work makes no call to the retired planner, old receipts remain readable after rotation, grants and lease expiry are enforced, conflicting lookups cannot both proceed, and unknown coordinator calls wait for one explicitly authorised retry.

## Role access and eight specialist approvals · 22 November 2026

Five local PostgreSQL tests passed in 23.686 seconds. Authenticated HTML and JSON returned all 108 catalogue entries for PMO Lead, Finance Lead, HR, CEO, CTO and Admin, and for ordinary PMO and Finance members. The checks compared intake eligibility, sent the PMO engagement command, rejected another department and another organisation, checked the API schema and verified native bid-file receipt reuse across HTML and JSON. File storage was simulated for the bid test.

Three focused PostgreSQL tests passed together in 535.134 seconds. A PMO member appointed to resolve a specific evidence packet could see the reports and resolve the correct revision. Their appointment did not create a business approval. Removing a member from the project ended access. The Finance recipient named in an approved HR disclosure could acknowledge its metadata. Other staff, including the Finance head, could neither acknowledge it nor open its raw staff rows.

On Railway, three synthetic workflows exercised all eight specialists with OpenRouter and private storage through the actual approval and receiving services. Nine report files passed digest readback across F02, F35 and P52. With only the original metadata available, all nine raised evidence gaps. The original F02 run held an invalid citation; a focused repeat passed after the model instructions were aligned with the existing validator. The ledger contains the original results, the separate appointments, the evidence lines and storage checks. [Specialist receipts](docs/design/specialist-role-acceptance.md)

First integrated Railway run `5b614961-9e3d-470c-98d2-23735250361b` finished with 124 of 126 tests passing. All 63 source-triggered processes reached their receiving records and all 47 interface and decision tests passed. Two original-work tests exceeded the detail read budget on a signed change and published payroll, leaving six process rows unverified in that run. An earlier local regression run also encountered a deadlock and leftover accounts when two suites shared a test database. The Railway runner gives each suite its own clean clone. The subsequent local integration and privacy runs passed 7/7 and 6/6 respectively. On the same synthetic records the current implementation used 594 queries for the signed change, 749 for published payroll and 769 for exceptional staff costs, within the existing 1,400-query limit.

Release matrix `3f368f36-544a-46a5-8f13-2044c410e7c6` passed all 85 record-level tests across four Railway PostgreSQL groups. All 108 process IDs had an authenticated page and API result for each of eight accounts. The native PMO group passed 29/29, Finance accounts 11/11, Finance operations 14/14 and original intakes 31/31. The separate interface and decision group passed 80/81 on this run. Its one failure expected a forbidden response when following a withdrawn HR tab. Since the earlier role design, Django hides that tab and returns 404 on direct access, while the API returns 403 for the known project. The updated regression checks the missing link, the hidden page and the surviving independent project permission. It passed locally in 2.885 seconds. [Record and role evidence](docs/design/role-process-acceptance.md)

OpenRouter follow-up `26c053b8-44e8-4500-a5a0-b9692e98cc37` passed 7/7 in 64.174 seconds, including the real F02 workflow, its current receipt under all eight staff accounts and six projection and disclosure checks. A03 and A05 returned supported findings on the validated total. A01 raised an evidence gap. The configured information and business decisions were made by distinct synthetic principals. All 12 report digests across successful cases matched their archived evidence. The test removed its private object versions and the temporary provider bindings were verified absent. [Detailed live outputs](docs/design/specialist-role-acceptance.md)

Final interface rerun `d500e234-d2f9-4677-a4fe-1226c48dc0ba` passed 81/81 in 587.145 seconds against Railway PostgreSQL. The catalogue, actual grants, invitations and eligibility, scoped reviews, revocation, protected source reuse, CSRF and API contracts passed. With the 85 passing record tests from `3f368f36-544a-46a5-8f13-2044c410e7c6`, all 166 distinct release checks passed across the five groups. The evidence generator accepted the two passing logs and produced exactly 108 unique rows. End-to-end provider receipts are recorded separately. These role checks send authenticated requests through Django templates, middleware and JSON endpoints. The hosted smoke checks covered health, sign-in, the public schema and unauthenticated denial.

## Native department stations · 21 November 2026

Railway design-acceptance checks deployment `468810e4-e724-4bf2-9781-646c81cdff41` ran three suites on separate isolated PostgreSQL databases with synthetic people, documents and provider responses. All 34 PMO, 15 Finance close and 14 Finance operations process IDs reached their recorded downstream models. PMO passed 12 tests in 231.474 seconds, close passed six in 389.879 seconds and operations passed 12 in 134.910 seconds. This baseline preceded the final consuming-command guards. An earlier local run exposed the closed factory definition in PostgreSQL; migration 0098 extended its source, policy and observed receipt checks.

Deployment `9d8906db-2b2f-4974-8e69-bc0d5a7f7135` repeated all 63 receivers with the specialist handoff checked inside the receiving business commands. PMO passed 12 tests in 246.454 seconds, Finance close passed six in 400.013 seconds and Finance operations passed 12 in 132.092 seconds.

Local PostgreSQL reproduced the competing-template bypass and then passed the corrected P11 test in 92.239 seconds. Two further F35 tests passed in 191.814 seconds. They exercised withdrawn or returned HR authorisation before capture and a second nominated Finance reader attempting to see the first reader’s completed receipt in the project and assistant. The routing-book check matched all 122 catalogue IDs.

Deployment `5c91669d-b7ce-4b35-b113-e389bbfb0156` exercised the PostgreSQL completion guard through workflow migration 0100. All 63 receivers passed again. The PMO and shared-controls group passed 27 tests in 881.413 seconds, Finance close passed six in 1,090.280 seconds and Finance operations passed 12 in 339.796 seconds. A separate two-case run on the same image used real OpenRouter specialist calls and the private bucket. P02 passed. F35 stopped before receipt because one or more readings lacked a verified report. Its first diagnostic did not distinguish malformed output, storage failure or an uncertain provider response. The following build prevents a reviewer from accepting a missing report and records the reader’s status on a failed synthetic check.

Deployment `44b5d9aa-0e71-478e-a4ad-dedf57b72ab6` passed the fresh F35 live case in 44.416 seconds using the actual model and private bucket. It verified each role’s report digest and the named Finance recipient’s native HR disclosure receipt. The model received two lines of synthetic request metadata. Business decisions still required the native HR and Finance services. The original paid call was never replayed. Locally, two PostgreSQL regressions passed in 46.464 seconds covering an intake still waiting for its worker and a missing or uncertain specialist report that even the designated reviewer cannot accept.

On the same deployment, 29 PMO and shared-controls tests passed in 713.948 seconds and 14 Finance operations tests passed in 321.629 seconds. The PMO evidence includes both P11 outcomes, a template claim and an owner resolution of competing versions. Finance operations included both F35 access regressions. The Finance close suite encountered two setup errors creating an existing synthetic username in its reused database. Its partial receipts are excluded from the release ledger. The runner now creates separate databases for each execution, checks the migrated template for test accounts and removes just the databases it created. Both temporary provider references were removed from the checks service and their absence verified.

Finance close rerun `ca2df73e-d7d7-42cd-adcd-3d7f5ae71e61` passed all six tests in 425.619 seconds on a fresh isolated database and verified all 15 expected receiving records. Combined release evidence covers 49 passing workflow and regression tests and 63 distinct process IDs. The two real-provider cases are counted separately. The release web and worker both started successfully. Both `/health/` and `/accounts/login/` returned HTTP 200. [Per-process receiving records](docs/design/native-department-receipts.md)

Local PostgreSQL passed 39 existing progression and engagement tests on a separate, clean test database. It uses database `test_psl_specialist_loop`, checks the Railway environment and runs paid tests at most once per deployment. Private test artifacts are deleted after readback. On a local test database use `python manage.py test tests.test_specialist_loop.SpecialistLoopTests`.

## Implemented acceptance evidence

- tests/test_application.py: staff HTTP decisions/signature/completion → actual services/PostgreSQL → immutable outbox → internal Finance adapter/inbox → cited synthetic agent run/audit, all sharing case/evidence IDs.
- WorkerConcurrencyTests.test_staff_handoff_survives_worker_process_exit_and_restart commits actual staff requests, starts a separate Python process that reserves a lease and exits, expires the persisted lease, then starts a fresh work_outbox process. It asserts one confirmed Finance receipt and a subsequent linked agent audit. This proves process-boundary recovery for that path; it does not establish every M1/FULL acceptance requirement.
- TransactionTestCase uses two actual PostgreSQL connections/threads to claim one intent once. Tests cover expired fencing, timeout after persisted acceptance, failed receipt lookup without dispatch, revoked-grant hold, semantic duplicate milestones and payload/receipt immutability.
- Exact costing sequence and separate quality decision; no premature signature; cost revision invalidation; policy revocation; supersession and reapproval without overwriting decision history.
- Per-record/action access, cross-case evidence exclusion, admin-without-grant denial, CSRF enforcement and template escaping. Source instructions stay quoted data and uncited provider output is rejected.
- All 57 components tested absent and invalid-draft activation; independently configured timezone/roles/source validators tested active, invalid, superseded, stale or revoked as applicable. Synthetic-to-operational mix is rejected. This is not a complete active/revoked golden fixture matrix for all 57; 53 semantic implementations remain unfinished.
- tests/test_finance_intake.py: exact decimal text and input lineage, unknown amount preservation, malformed/duplicate inputs, configured conventions, currency/period mismatch, tampered artifact hash, source-policy holds, actual staff capture screen and Finance preparation intake. Source validation never claims accounts/reconciliation success.
- Document reviews bind one immutable version; support response/resolution/reopen preserve history and never create a corrective credit.

These environment failures were resolved and are not passing application tests.
